# Commission Decision of 20 February 2023 approving, on behalf of the European Union, the modification of Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part 2023/C 92/01

|  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 10.3.2023 | EN | Official Journal of the European Union | C 92/1 |

## COMMISSION DECISION

## of 20 February 2023

## approving, on behalf of the European Union, the modification of Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part

(2023/C 92/01)

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION,

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union,

Having regard to Council Decision (EU) 2016/838 of 23 May 2016 on the conclusion, on behalf of the European Union, of the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part [^ntr1-C_2023092EN.01000101-E0001], and in particular Article 3 thereof,

Whereas:

- (1) The Agreement between the European Union and Georgia on protection of geographical indications of agricultural products and foodstuffs [^ntr2-C_2023092EN.01000101-E0002] entered into force on 1 April 2012.

- (2) That Agreement was subsequently incorporated into the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part [^ntr3-C_2023092EN.01000101-E0003] (‘the Agreement’), which entered into force on 1 July 2016.

- (3) Article 179 of the Agreement establishes the Geographical Indications Sub-Committee that is to see to the proper functioning of the sub-section on geographical indications of the Agreement and is responsible for, among others, modifying the lists of protected geographical indications set out in Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Agreement.

- (4) The Geographical Indications Sub-Committee examined the list of newly registered geographical indications proposed for inclusion in Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Agreement, as well as proposed corrections and removals of names that are currently listed in those Annexes but are no longer protected in the Parties. The examination and internal procedures of the Parties have been completed. In the Union, interested parties have not raised any objections against the geographical indication proposed by Georgia for protection under the Agreement. Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Agreement may therefore be modified. Those modifications should be approved on behalf of the Union,

HAS DECIDED AS FOLLOWS:

#### Article 1

The modifications of Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part, set out in the draft Decision of the Geographical Indications Sub-Committee, are hereby approved on behalf of the European Union.

The draft Decision of the Geographical Indications Sub-Committee is set out in the Annex to this Decision.

#### Article 2

The Head of the Union Delegation to the Geographical Indications Sub-Committee is authorised to adopt the decision of that Sub-Committee on behalf of the European Union.

#### Article 3

After its adoption, the decision of the Geographical Indications Sub-Committee shall be published in *the Official Journal of the European Union*.

Done at Brussels, 20 February 2023.

***For the Commission***

**Janusz WOJCIECHOWSKI**

***Member of the Commission***

[^ntr1-C_2023092EN.01000101-E0001]: [OJ L 141, 28.5.2016, p. 26](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2016_141_R_TOC).

[^ntr2-C_2023092EN.01000101-E0002]: [OJ L 93, 30.3.2012, p. 3](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2012_093_R_TOC).

[^ntr3-C_2023092EN.01000101-E0003]: [OJ L 261, 30.8.2014, p. 4](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2014_261_R_TOC).

## ANNEX

## DECISION No 1/2023 OF THE GEOGRAPHICAL INDICATIONS SUB-COMMITTEE

## of xx xxxx 2023

## modifying Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part

THE GEOGRAPHICAL INDICATIONS SUB-COMMITTEE,

Having regard to the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part, and in particular Article 179(3)(b) thereof,

Whereas:

- (1) The Agreement between the European Union and Georgia on protection of geographical indications of agricultural products and foodstuffs entered into force on 1 April 2012.

- (2) That Agreement was subsequently incorporated into the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part (hereinafter ‘the Agreement’), which entered into force on 1 July 2016.

- (3) Article 171 of the Agreement provides for the possibility to add new geographical indications to be protected in Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D thereto after having completed the objection procedure and after having examined a summary of the specifications as referred to in Article 170(3) and (4) thereof, to the satisfaction of both Parties.

- (4) That procedure and examination have been completed and Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D may therefore be modified,

HAS DECIDED AS FOLLOWS:

#### Article 1

Annexes XVII-C and XVII-D to the Association Agreement between the European Union and the European Atomic Energy Community and their Member States, of the one part, and Georgia, of the other part are replaced by the text in the Annex to this Decision.

#### Article 2

This Decision shall enter into force on the day of its adoption.

Done at Brussels, the xx xxxx 2023.

**For the Geographical Indications Sub-Committee**

***The Sub-Committee Secretaries***

***EU Secretary***

**Gesa WESSELER**

***The Chair (Head of Georgian Delegation)***

**Soso GIORGADZE**

***Georgia Secretary***

**Manana PRUIDZE**
